- **data-minimization-desk**: make field-level necessity determinations tied to a named purpose, choose pseudonymization, tokenization, aggregation, or generalization with the key custody that keeps the technique meaningful, run re-identification assessments naming the auxiliary data considered, and hold the boundary between pseudonymized data that stays in scope and anonymous data that leaves it. use for data minimization reviews, field necessity audits, de-identification and anonymization design, k-anonymity and differential privacy choices, tokenization and key custody, analytics and model training datasets, and over-c… ([MadewellRD/skills-lab](https://github.com/MadewellRD/skills-lab/tree/HEAD/dist/skills/privacy-data-protection-command-desk/data-minimization-desk) · ★2 · 4 copies)

- **data-minimization-review**: Reduce personal-data collection and persistence to what is necessary for approved product purposes while preserving required functionality and evidence. ([Dadmin88/hermes-profile-packs](https://github.com/Dadmin88/hermes-profile-packs/tree/HEAD/hermes-agency/profiles/agency-privacy-engineer/skills/data-minimization-review) · ★49)
- **data-minimization-review**: Use when reviewing an existing system, feature, or dataset for excess personal data. Covers field level collection audits, retention review, log and analytics scrubbing, and turning findings into drop, mask, or shorten decisions. Use when a product ships new fields, when a privacy review flags overcollection, or when preparing evidence that only necessary data is kept. ([mizazhaider-ceh/AgentArsenal](https://github.com/mizazhaider-ceh/AgentArsenal/tree/HEAD/skills/privacy/data-minimization/sub/data-minimization-review))

- **data-mining**: Data Mining. Unsupervised pattern discovery - clustering and customer segmentation (k-means, k-medoids, hierarchical, DBSCAN, HDBSCAN, Gaussian mixtures), association rules and market basket analysis (Apriori, FP-Growth, support, confidence, lift), anomaly and outlier detection (Isolation Forest, LOF, one-class SVM, robust z-scores, autoencoders), dimensionality reduction for exploration (PCA, t-SNE, UMAP), sequence mining, recommenders and text mining. Use for segmentation, clustering, unsupervised learning, basket analysis, fraud or anomaly screening, topic discovery. ([Harrypatria/data-mining-ml](https://github.com/Harrypatria/data-mining-ml/tree/HEAD/skills/data-mining) · ★2)
- **data-mining**: Extract patterns and insights from large datasets using association rules, clustering, classification, and anomaly detection. Use for market basket analysis, customer segmentation, fraud detection, pattern discovery, predictive modeling, and knowledge extraction from structured and unstructured data. ([shivamchopra7/claude-skill-directory](https://github.com/shivamchopra7/claude-skill-directory/tree/HEAD/skills/analysis/data-mining))

- **data-mining-engine**: 珍妮纺织机·数据挖掘引擎。SuperMickey 全链路的情报层（Stage -2）， 在创意主题生成之前运行。五个 Agent 串行流水线：商品情报采集员 → 用户评价矿工 → 竞品侦察员 → 交叉验证官 → 档案装订员，产出《商品情报 档案》（含商品图 manifest）与六张下游摘要卡。使用时机：社媒营销/商品 推广类任务，或任何需要商品真实情报支撑的创作任务。 ([geniusdapeng-collab/hyperreality-system](https://github.com/geniusdapeng-collab/hyperreality-system/tree/HEAD/vendor/supermickey/hyperreality-system/engines/data-mining-engine) · ★11 · 2 copies)

- **data-privacy**: Builds privacy into the system rather than onto it: an inventory of the personal data actually held, a lawful and stated purpose for each field, minimisation and retention with deletion that works, access control and audit, subject rights implemented as features, third party sharing, consent where it applies, and the rules that keep personal data out of logs, evidence and analytics. Use before collecting a new field, and whenever personal data crosses a boundary. - **data-privacy**: Use when a ticket handles personal data — collecting a new field about a person, exporting or deleting a user's data, retention and anonymisation, analytics events, sharing with a third party — and the change must minimise what is collected, protect it in storage and logs, honour deletion and access requests, and be explainable to a regulator. Invoke for GDPR/CCPA-style work, "add a tracking event", "delete my account", "export user data", or any new column that describes a person.
